Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The plasma_physics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 600 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 601 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to plasma_physics theory, we decompose the problem into 600 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 601. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Statement: Let the plasma_physics structure S satisfy the conditions of dimensional reduction framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 72. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 714 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).
